# IIIT Lucknow - Images & Media Assets Catalog

> **Directory:** `assets/images/`  
> **Total Registered Images:** 969 files  
> **Total Disk Storage:** 177.60 MB  
> **Status:** Downloaded, deduplicated, verified, and mapped to source pages and semantic categories.

This document provides a comprehensive catalog of all image assets (excluding logos and documents) extracted across all pages, subpages, faculty profiles, galleries, and news archives from the official website of the Indian Institute of Information Technology, Lucknow (https://iiitl.ac.in/).

---

## Category Overview

| Category | Asset Count | Description & Scope |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Leadership, Faculty & Staff Profiles** | 208 | Official portraits and headshots of the Director, Founding Director, Deans, Faculty Members, Registrars, and Staff. |
| **Campus Architecture & Infrastructure** | 65 | High-resolution architectural photography of the permanent campus, academic block, hostel facilities, laboratories, and grounds. |
| **Convocations & Institutional Ceremonies** | 93 | Photographs from official convocations, national festivals (Republic Day, Independence Day, Gandhi Jayanti), and foundation days. |
| **Academic Programs, Conferences & Posters** | 51 | Official flyers, academic conference banners (ANTIC), FDP announcements, PhD call posters, and symposium graphics. |
| **Student Life, Clubs & Festivals** | 72 | Student council, technical & cultural fests (Equinox), E-Summit (Enspire), dance & music societies, and sports meets. |
| **Press & Media Coverage** | 83 | Scans of regional and national newspaper coverage highlighting placement records, academic achievements, and institutional milestones. |
| **UI Assets, Badges & Decorative Graphics** | 64 | Animated notices, system icons, transportation/facility icons, and theme presentation graphics. |
| **General Campus & Institutional Media** | 333 | Miscellaneous photography, institutional event galleries, and historical milestone documentation. |
